1- When you connect an OAUTH account and want to replace the remote vault with the local one, the spinner goes forever (over 2 minutes). We need to optimize this for all OAUTH flows, maybe we are deleting all files one by one? Did we already change this flow to use a pMap? I do not know, we need to investigate. The extension doesn't have a spinner, but also happens, and I am willing to bet mobile too.

2- When syncing via the settings screen (after connecting to google drive) in the extension, we now block the screen after the latest changes (which is good), but when we finish it still keeps the "syncing"! ![Screenshot](still-sync.png) Please try to open the linked image. It also happens if I do the same with dropbox.

3- If I try to dropbox after not touching the extension for a while, I get

> Uncaught (in promise) Error: Dropbox upload failed (HTTP 429)
